2026-11-11T05:25:04.8245166Z E           ## 1. Runtime Topology & System Boundaries
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8245602Z E           
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8245981Z E           ### 1.1 Process Architecture & Service Boundaries
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8246933Z E           - **Process Model:** Single-process Python 3.12+ ASGI application powered by Starlette/FastAPI, served via Uvicorn.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8249746Z E           - **Network Interface Binding:** Strictly bound to loopback `127.0.0.1` (or `[::1]`). Startup refuses binding to any wildcard (`0.0.0.0`) or external non-loopback IP unless both `REDLOOP_ALLOW_REMOTE=1` is explicitly set AND the operator token capability is fully provisioned.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8251740Z E           - **TCP Port Invariants:**
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8252637Z E             - **Port 8080:** Unified Command Center, REST API Gateway, WebSocket Terminal Server, SOC Workbench SPA.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8253829Z E             - **Port 8765:** Operations Dashboard and Read-Only Cryptographic Reporting Server.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8254551Z E           - **Data Directory Layout:**
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8255849Z E             - `data/`: SQLite databases (`audit.sqlite`, `chats.sqlite`, `osint_cache.sqlite`), encrypted credentials keystore, and raw tool evidence files (`data/evidence/*.raw`).
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8257764Z E             - `logs/`: Application diagnostic logs and operational traces.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8258957Z E             - `artifacts/`: Evaluated test metrics, holdout datasets, JUnit XML baselines, route snapshots, and verification ledgers.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8259940Z E           
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8260321Z E           ### 1.2 Database Schemas & Storage Engines
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8261307Z E           REDLOOP utilizes local SQLite storage with WAL (Write-Ahead Logging) enabled and strict append-only constraints:
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8262232Z E           1. **Audit Ledger (`audit_log`):**
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8262788Z E              - Columns: `id`, `timestamp`, `event_type`, `action`, `actor`, `status`, `target`, `metadata_json`, `prev_hash`, `event_hash`.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8263468Z E              - Hash Chain: HMAC-SHA256 linking each event to the preceding event hash.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8264128Z E              - Database Enforced Triggers: `BEFORE UPDATE` and `BEFORE DELETE` triggers reject mutations with `RAISE(ABORT)`.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8264757Z E           2. **Case & Incident Management (`cases`, `incidents`, `evidence`):**
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8265384Z E              - Entity tracking, provenance chains, MITRE ATT&CK tactic/technique mappings, and threat actor indicators.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8266112Z E           3. **OSINT Verification Cache (`osint_cache`):**
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8266722Z E              - Keyed by `(url_or_target, query_type)`, storing status codes, response snippets, headers, timestamps, and 24-hour default TTL.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8267971Z E           4. **SOAR Capabilities & Playbook State (`playbook_approval_capabilities`, `playbook_execution_history`):**
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8268753Z E              - Cryptographic fingerprints of operator tokens, stage tracking (`READY -> RUNNING -> COMPLETED / CANCELLED`).
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8269283Z E

2026-11-11T05:25:04.8269876Z E           ## 2. Core Security Invariants
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8270138Z E           
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8270469Z E           ### S1. Terminal & Command Scope Gate (`redloop/assessment/terminal_engine.py`)
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8271213Z E           - **Tokenization:** Commands parsed strictly via `shlex.split()` without spawning an intermediate shell (`cmd.exe` or `powershell.exe`).
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8272275Z E           - **Metacharacter Defense:** Any command containing shell operators (`;`, `&`, `|`, `` ` ``, `$()`, `>`, `<`, newlines) is rejected immediately with return code `126` (BLOCKED) unless allowlisted.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8273320Z E           - **Allowlisted Binaries:** Only approved network utilities (e.g., `nmap`, `curl`, `wget`, `nc`, `ping`, `ssh`, `dig`, `nuclei`) may target hosts.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8274534Z E           - **Target & Port Extraction:** Every host, IP, URL, and explicit port is extracted from command arguments. Default nmap top-1000 requires `ports=None` in scope; otherwise explicit `-p` matching authorized ports is mandatory.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8276027Z E           - **Anti-Obfuscation:** Decodes decimal IPv4 (e.g. `2130706433`), octal/hex IPv4, bracketed IPv6 `[::1]:port`, and URL userinfo (`***evil.com`).
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8277358Z E           - **DNS Pinning:** Resolves target hostnames and validates that the resolved IP matches authorized scope (`validate_resolved_ip`). Zero packets or subprocesses are executed on denied commands.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8278132Z E           
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8278445Z E           ### S2. LLM Tool-Calling Hardening (`redloop/assessment/ai_tool_agent.py`)
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8279035Z E           - **Untrusted Input Assumption:** All tool outputs and LLM outputs are treated as untrusted data.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8280228Z E           - **Data Delimiters:** Tool results are enclosed within strict `<untrusted_tool_output>` blocks, accompanied by a system prompt invariant instructing the model never to parse embedded text as instructions.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8281377Z E           - **Safety Limits:** Maximum 8 execution steps per turn, 120s wall-clock timeout, per-call timeouts, and output truncation limits.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8282442Z E           - **Secrets Redaction:** Regex-based sanitization strips API keys (`gsk_*`, `sk-*`, `AIza*`, *** before evidence files are committed to disk or rendered to UI.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8283091Z E           
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8283417Z E           ### S3. SOAR Operator Authentication (`redloop/assessment/soar_engine.py`)
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8284008Z E           - **Operator Token Generation:** Generated via `secrets.token_urlsafe(32)` at initial setup.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8284632Z E           - **Fingerprint Storage:** Only the SHA-256 hash is persisted in the capabilities table.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8285485Z E           - **Approval Endpoint:** `POST /api/soc/playbooks/{id}/approve` requires `X-Operator-Token` header, matching step ID, same-origin validation, and rate limiting.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8286571Z E           - **Audited Approvals:** Approvals log the operator identity from server configuration, token fingerprint (first 8 hex characters), step ID, and timestamp.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8287429Z E           
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8287773Z E           ### S4. Immutable Audit Ledger (`redloop/audit.py`, `redloop/audit_checkpoint.py`)
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8288504Z E           - **Database-Level Protection:** SQLite triggers abort any `UPDATE` or `DELETE` attempt.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8289381Z E           - **External Checkpointing:** `AuditCheckpointManager` generates detached HMAC-SHA256 signatures over state hashes, anchored via `redloop audit anchor --confirm`.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8290073Z E           
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8290372Z E           ### S5. Secrets & Credential Hygiene (`redloop/provider_gateway.py`)
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8291082Z E           - **Zero Committed Secrets:** Repository scanned for provider tokens and cryptographic keys.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8292902Z E           - **Multi-Tenant User Isolation:** Keystore lookups isolate provider credentials by `user_id`; environment fallback is restricted strictly to the local system operator (`user_id == 'default'`).
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8295147Z E           - **Secret Rotation:** CLI command `redloop scope rotate-secret` provisions new cryptographic keys, updates environment configurations, and re-signs scope manifests.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8295945Z E           
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8296307Z E           ### S6. Loopback Binding Invariant (`redloop/dashboard.py`, `redloop/unified_app.py`)
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8297204Z E           - Server factories refuse initialization if binding host is not `127.0.0.1` or `::1`, unless elevated bypass is configured.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8297786Z E           
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8298070Z E           ### 5.1 Transport Security & Anti-SSRF (`redloop/transport.py`)
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8299158Z E           - **Subnet Filtering:** Blocks all requests to RFC-1918 private subnets, RFC-6598 carrier-grade NAT (`100.64.0.0/10`), loopback (`127.0.0.0/8`, `::1`), link-local, multicast, and cloud metadata services (`169.254.169.254`, `metadata.google.internal`, `instance-data`).
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8300597Z E           - **DNS Rebinding Defense:** Validates and pins resolved IP addresses at socket connect time via `HardenedSyncBackend` and `HardenedAsyncBackend`.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8301664Z E           - **Redirect Security:** Redirects disabled by default (`follow_redirects=False`); if enabled, redirect locations are strictly re-validated against subnet blocklists.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8302362Z E           
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8302545Z E           ---
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8302725Z E           
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8302951Z E           ## 3. Provider Inference & Failover Hierarchy
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8303240Z E           
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8303422Z E           ```mermaid
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8303633Z E           flowchart TD

2026-11-11T05:25:04.8313696Z E           ## 4. Evaluation Framework & Statistical Metrics
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8313995Z E           
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8314258Z E           ### 4.1 Statistical Formulation & Wilson Confidence Intervals
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8314886Z E           All model evaluations report raw counts and 95% confidence intervals calculated via the Wilson score interval formula:
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8315431Z E           
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8315985Z E           $$\hat{p} = \frac{n_\text{succ}}{n}, \quad w^\pm = \frac{\hat{p} + \frac{z^2}{2n} \pm z \sqrt{\frac{\hat{p}(1-\hat{p})}{n} + \frac{z^2}{4n^2}}}{1 + \frac{z^2}{n}} \quad (z = 1.95996)$$
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8317352Z E           
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8317780Z E           ### 4.2 Multi-Factor OSINT Confidence Calculation
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8318729Z E           Confidence scores for threat intelligence and external footprints are computed deterministically:
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8319332Z E           
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8319572Z E           $$C = 0.25S + 0.25D + 0.20I + 0.10F + 0.10P + 0.10X$$
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8319904Z E           - $S$: Source corroboration count
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8320228Z E           - $D$: Domain/platform authority score
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8320561Z E           - $I$: Identity/evidence consistency
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8320906Z E           - $F$: Freshness decay factor ($e^{-\lambda \Delta t}$)
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8321254Z E           - $P$: Pattern / regex certainty
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8321576Z E           - $X$: Absence of contradictory negative signals
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8321874Z E           
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8322124Z E           ### 4.3 Operational Safety Targets & Enforced Floors
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8322522Z E           | Metric | Definition | Baseline Floor | Target | Enforcement |
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8322896Z E           | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8323516Z E           | **Scope Breach Rate (SBR)** | Out-of-scope executions / total operations | $0.0000$ | $0.0000$ | Ratchet test (abort on $>0$) |
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8324339Z E           | **Fabrication Rate (FBR)** | Mock or invented results in product paths | $0.0000$ | $0.0000$ | Ratchet test (abort on $>0$) |
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8325320Z E           | **Intent Holdout Accuracy** | Accuracy on held-out intent dataset | $\ge 0.9000$ | $\ge 0.9400$ | Wilson 95% lower bound |
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8326092Z E           | **Holdout Macro-F1** | Unweighted mean of category F1 scores | $\ge 0.8500$ | $\ge 0.9000$ | Ratchet test |
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8326849Z E           | **Composite Quality Index ($Q$)** | Multi-factor system quality score | $\ge 0.7500$ | $\ge 0.8500$ | `/api/metrics/quality` |
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8327717Z E           
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8328198Z E           Held-out dataset immutability is strictly enforced (`tests/fixtures/eval/intent_holdout_v1.json` is marked read-only).
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8328735Z E

2026-11-11T05:25:04.8764557Z E           
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8764958Z E           To maintain technical integrity, REDLOOP explicitly specifies what the platform does **NOT** do:
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8766077Z E           1. **No Cloud Telemetry or Background Sync:** REDLOOP operates entirely locally. It does not transmit telemetry, analytics, crash dumps, or user queries to external cloud servers.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8767563Z E           2. **No Multi-Tenant User Authentication Beyond Operator Token:** The application is built for single-operator local usage. It does not provide web login screens, OAuth user workflows, or multi-tenant database partitioning.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8768990Z E           3. **No Weaponized Exploit Generation:** REDLOOP does not produce weaponized zero-day exploits, memory corruption payloads, or evasion tools designed to bypass defensive security systems.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8770321Z E           4. **No Fake or Synthetic Benchmarking:** Performance metrics, latency percentiles, and confidence scores are calculated exclusively from empirical observations and mathematically bounded formulas.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8771676Z E           5. **No Bypass of Scope Controls:** Scope validation is machine-enforced and fail-closed. No model hallucination, conversational prompt, or tool override can bypass the cryptographic scope manifest.

2026-11-11T05:25:04.8983277Z     ## 1. Runtime Topology & System Boundaries
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8983355Z     
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8983482Z     ### 1.1 Process Architecture & Service Boundaries
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8983836Z     - **Process Model:** Single-process Python 3.12+ ASGI application powered by Starlette/FastAPI, served via Uvicorn.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8984680Z     - **Network Interface Binding:** Strictly bound to loopback `127.0.0.1` (or `[::1]`). Startup refuses binding to any wildcard (`0.0.0.0`) or external non-loopback IP unless both `REDLOOP_ALLOW_REMOTE=1` is explicitly set AND the operator token capability is fully provisioned.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8984778Z     - **TCP Port Invariants:**
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8985079Z       - **Port 8080:** Unified Command Center, REST API Gateway, WebSocket Terminal Server, SOC Workbench SPA.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8985317Z       - **Port 8765:** Operations Dashboard and Read-Only Cryptographic Reporting Server.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8985417Z     - **Data Directory Layout:**
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8985931Z       - `data/`: SQLite databases (`audit.sqlite`, `chats.sqlite`, `osint_cache.sqlite`), encrypted credentials keystore, and raw tool evidence files (`data/evidence/*.raw`).
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8986090Z       - `logs/`: Application diagnostic logs and operational traces.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8986459Z       - `artifacts/`: Evaluated test metrics, holdout datasets, JUnit XML baselines, route snapshots, and verification ledgers.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8986537Z     
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8986650Z     ### 1.2 Database Schemas & Storage Engines
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8986991Z     REDLOOP utilizes local SQLite storage with WAL (Write-Ahead Logging) enabled and strict append-only constraints:
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8987210Z     1. **Audit Ledger (`audit_log`):**
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8987558Z        - Columns: `id`, `timestamp`, `event_type`, `action`, `actor`, `status`, `target`, `metadata_json`, `prev_hash`, `event_hash`.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8987757Z        - Hash Chain: HMAC-SHA256 linking each event to the preceding event hash.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8988092Z        - Database Enforced Triggers: `BEFORE UPDATE` and `BEFORE DELETE` triggers reject mutations with `RAISE(ABORT)`.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8988273Z     2. **Case & Incident Management (`cases`, `incidents`, `evidence`):**
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8988587Z        - Entity tracking, provenance chains, MITRE ATT&CK tactic/technique mappings, and threat actor indicators.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8988715Z     3. **OSINT Verification Cache (`osint_cache`):**
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8989204Z        - Keyed by `(url_or_target, query_type)`, storing status codes, response snippets, headers, timestamps, and 24-hour default TTL.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8989527Z     4. **SOAR Capabilities & Playbook State (`playbook_approval_capabilities`, `playbook_execution_history`):**
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8989870Z        - Cryptographic fingerprints of operator tokens, stage tracking (`READY -> RUNNING -> COMPLETED / CANCELLED`).
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8989944Z     
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8990027Z     ---
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8990098Z     
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8990198Z     ## 2. Core Security Invariants
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8990269Z     
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8990482Z     ### S1. Terminal & Command Scope Gate (`redloop/assessment/terminal_engine.py`)
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8990892Z     - **Tokenization:** Commands parsed strictly via `shlex.split()` without spawning an intermediate shell (`cmd.exe` or `powershell.exe`).
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8991425Z     - **Metacharacter Defense:** Any command containing shell operators (`;`, `&`, `|`, `` ` ``, `$()`, `>`, `<`, newlines) is rejected immediately with return code `126` (BLOCKED) unless allowlisted.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8991875Z     - **Allowlisted Binaries:** Only approved network utilities (e.g., `nmap`, `curl`, `wget`, `nc`, `ping`, `ssh`, `dig`, `nuclei`) may target hosts.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8992952Z     - **Target & Port Extraction:** Every host, IP, URL, and explicit port is extracted from command arguments. Default nmap top-1000 requires `ports=None` in scope; otherwise explicit `-p` matching authorized ports is mandatory.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8993644Z     - **Anti-Obfuscation:** Decodes decimal IPv4 (e.g. `2130706433`), octal/hex IPv4, bracketed IPv6 `[::1]:port`, and URL userinfo (`***evil.com`).
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8994239Z     - **DNS Pinning:** Resolves target hostnames and validates that the resolved IP matches authorized scope (`validate_resolved_ip`). Zero packets or subprocesses are executed on denied commands.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8994320Z     
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8994521Z     ### S2. LLM Tool-Calling Hardening (`redloop/assessment/ai_tool_agent.py`)
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8994800Z     - **Untrusted Input Assumption:** All tool outputs and LLM outputs are treated as untrusted data.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8995448Z     - **Data Delimiters:** Tool results are enclosed within strict `<untrusted_tool_output>` blocks, accompanied by a system prompt invariant instructing the model never to parse embedded text as instructions.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8995836Z     - **Safety Limits:** Maximum 8 execution steps per turn, 120s wall-clock timeout, per-call timeouts, and output truncation limits.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8996355Z     - **Secrets Redaction:** Regex-based sanitization strips API keys (`gsk_*`, `sk-*`, `AIza*`, *** before evidence files are committed to disk or rendered to UI.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8996438Z     
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8996637Z     ### S3. SOAR Operator Authentication (`redloop/assessment/soar_engine.py`)
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8996904Z     - **Operator Token Generation:** Generated via `secrets.token_urlsafe(32)` at initial setup.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.8997419Z     - **Fingerprint Storage:** Only the SHA-256 hash is persisted in the capabilities table.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9010753Z     - **Approval Endpoint:** `POST /api/soc/playbooks/{id}/approve` requires `X-Operator-Token` header, matching step ID, same-origin validation, and rate limiting.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9011327Z     - **Audited Approvals:** Approvals log the operator identity from server configuration, token fingerprint (first 8 hex characters), step ID, and timestamp.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9011407Z     
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9011648Z     ### S4. Immutable Audit Ledger (`redloop/audit.py`, `redloop/audit_checkpoint.py`)
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9011909Z     - **Database-Level Protection:** SQLite triggers abort any `UPDATE` or `DELETE` attempt.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9012633Z     - **External Checkpointing:** `AuditCheckpointManager` generates detached HMAC-SHA256 signatures over state hashes, anchored via `redloop audit anchor --confirm`.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9012728Z     
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9012921Z     ### S5. Secrets & Credential Hygiene (`redloop/provider_gateway.py`)
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9013188Z     - **Zero Committed Secrets:** Repository scanned for provider tokens and cryptographic keys.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9013798Z     - **Multi-Tenant User Isolation:** Keystore lookups isolate provider credentials by `user_id`; environment fallback is restricted strictly to the local system operator (`user_id == 'default'`).
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9014322Z     - **Secret Rotation:** CLI command `redloop scope rotate-secret` provisions new cryptographic keys, updates environment configurations, and re-signs scope manifests.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9014407Z     
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9014642Z     ### S6. Loopback Binding Invariant (`redloop/dashboard.py`, `redloop/unified_app.py`)
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9015016Z     - Server factories refuse initialization if binding host is not `127.0.0.1` or `::1`, unless elevated bypass is configured.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9015093Z     
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9015268Z     ### 5.1 Transport Security & Anti-SSRF (`redloop/transport.py`)
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9016083Z     - **Subnet Filtering:** Blocks all requests to RFC-1918 private subnets, RFC-6598 carrier-grade NAT (`100.64.0.0/10`), loopback (`127.0.0.0/8`, `::1`), link-local, multicast, and cloud metadata services (`169.254.169.254`, `metadata.google.internal`, `instance-data`).
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9016650Z     - **DNS Rebinding Defense:** Validates and pins resolved IP addresses at socket connect time via `HardenedSyncBackend` and `HardenedAsyncBackend`.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9017412Z     - **Redirect Security:** Redirects disabled by default (`follow_redirects=False`); if enabled, redirect locations are strictly re-validated against subnet blocklists.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9017499Z     
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9017579Z     ---
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9017656Z     
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9017780Z     ## 3. Provider Inference & Failover Hierarchy
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9017857Z     
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9017939Z     ```mermaid
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9018025Z     flowchart TD
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9018190Z         Q[User Prompt] --> O[Ollama: qwen3:8b (Local / Offline)]
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9018303Z         O -->|Success| R[Render Verified Answer]
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9018491Z         O -->|ConnectionRefused / Unavailable| G[Groq: qwen/qwen3.8-27b]
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9018579Z         G -->|Success| R
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9018814Z         G -->|Auth / RateLimit Failure| OR[OpenRouter: meta-llama/llama-3.3-70b-instruct]
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9018901Z         OR -->|Success| R
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9019109Z         OR -->|All Cloud Providers Unavailable| DET[Offline Deterministic Engine]
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9019228Z         DET --> BM25[BM25 RAG Knowledge Retrieval]
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9019464Z         BM25 -->|Confidence >= 0.20| KB[68+ Vetted Knowledge Packs (Citations: Sources: [title])]
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9019743Z         BM25 -->|Uncovered Question| REF[Honest Offline Refusal: 'No vetted offline answer for this.']
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9019821Z     ```
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9019901Z     
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9020230Z     1. **Primary: Ollama (`qwen3:8b`)** — Local daemon, zero cloud egress.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9020602Z     2. **Secondary: Groq (`qwen/qwen3.8-27b`)** — Low-latency cloud inference when local daemon is stopped.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9020949Z     3. **Tertiary: OpenRouter (`meta-llama/llama-3.3-70b-instruct`)** — High-capacity fallback.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9021855Z     4. **Terminal Fallback: Offline Deterministic Engine (`redloop/assessment/orchestrator.py`)** — Grounded in 68+ curated security packs (`data/knowledge/seed/`) indexed by BM25. Strictly refuses unvetted answers rather than hallucinating.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9021936Z     
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9022014Z     ---
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9022095Z     
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9022226Z     ## 4. Evaluation Framework & Statistical Metrics
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9022299Z     
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9022590Z     ### 4.1 Statistical Formulation & Wilson Confidence Intervals
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9022959Z     All model evaluations report raw counts and 95% confidence intervals calculated via the Wilson score interval formula:
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9023038Z     
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9023526Z     $$\hat{p} = \frac{n_\text{succ}}{n}, \quad w^\pm = \frac{\hat{p} + \frac{z^2}{2n} \pm z \sqrt{\frac{\hat{p}(1-\hat{p})}{n} + \frac{z^2}{4n^2}}}{1 + \frac{z^2}{n}} \quad (z = 1.95996)$$
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9023613Z     
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9023742Z     ### 4.2 Multi-Factor OSINT Confidence Calculation
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9024032Z     Confidence scores for threat intelligence and external footprints are computed deterministically:
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9024110Z     
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9024228Z     $$C = 0.25S + 0.25D + 0.20I + 0.10F + 0.10P + 0.10X$$
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9024336Z     - $S$: Source corroboration count
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9024442Z     - $D$: Domain/platform authority score
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9024547Z     - $I$: Identity/evidence consistency
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9024695Z     - $F$: Freshness decay factor ($e^{-\lambda \Delta t}$)
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9024794Z     - $P$: Pattern / regex certainty
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9024917Z     - $X$: Absence of contradictory negative signals
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9024989Z     
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9025123Z     ### 4.3 Operational Safety Targets & Enforced Floors
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9025409Z     | Metric | Definition | Baseline Floor | Target | Enforcement |
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9025505Z     | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9025875Z     | **Scope Breach Rate (SBR)** | Out-of-scope executions / total operations | $0.0000$ | $0.0000$ | Ratchet test (abort on $>0$) |
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9026219Z     | **Fabrication Rate (FBR)** | Mock or invented results in product paths | $0.0000$ | $0.0000$ | Ratchet test (abort on $>0$) |
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9026562Z     | **Intent Holdout Accuracy** | Accuracy on held-out intent dataset | $\ge 0.9000$ | $\ge 0.9400$ | Wilson 95% lower bound |
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9026854Z     | **Holdout Macro-F1** | Unweighted mean of category F1 scores | $\ge 0.8500$ | $\ge 0.9000$ | Ratchet test |
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9027325Z     | **Composite Quality Index ($Q$)** | Multi-factor system quality score | $\ge 0.7500$ | $\ge 0.8500$ | `/api/metrics/quality` |
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9027411Z     
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9027775Z     Held-out dataset immutability is strictly enforced (`tests/fixtures/eval/intent_holdout_v1.json` is marked read-only).
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9027853Z     
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9027930Z     ---

2026-11-11T05:25:04.9210876Z     ## 7. Operational Honesty & Architectural Non-Goals
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9210959Z     
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9211245Z     To maintain technical integrity, REDLOOP explicitly specifies what the platform does **NOT** do:
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9211804Z     1. **No Cloud Telemetry or Background Sync:** REDLOOP operates entirely locally. It does not transmit telemetry, analytics, crash dumps, or user queries to external cloud servers.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9212514Z     2. **No Multi-Tenant User Authentication Beyond Operator Token:** The application is built for single-operator local usage. It does not provide web login screens, OAuth user workflows, or multi-tenant database partitioning.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9213108Z     3. **No Weaponized Exploit Generation:** REDLOOP does not produce weaponized zero-day exploits, memory corruption payloads, or evasion tools designed to bypass defensive security systems.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9213747Z     4. **No Fake or Synthetic Benchmarking:** Performance metrics, latency percentiles, and confidence scores are calculated exclusively from empirical observations and mathematically bounded formulas.
2026-11-11T05:25:04.9214364Z     5. **No Bypass of Scope Controls:** Scope validation is machine-enforced and fail-closed. No model hallucination, conversational prompt, or tool override can bypass the cryptographic scope manifest.
